  4815     223  theorem oddSteps_all_ones_le : ∀ (i a m : ℕ), i ≤ m → oddSteps (3 ^ a * 2 ^ m - 1) i = i := by
  4816     224    intro i
  4817     225    induction i with
  4818     226    | zero => intro a m _; simp [oddSteps]
  4819     227    | succ i ih =>
  4820     228      intro a m hm
  4821     229      obtain ⟨m', rfl⟩ : ∃ m', m = m' + 1 := ⟨m - 1, by omega⟩
  4822     230      have hM : 3 ^ a * 2 ^ (m'+1) = 2 * (3 ^ a * 2 ^ m') := by ring
  4823     231      have hpos : 1 ≤ 3 ^ a * 2 ^ m' := Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.mpr (by positivity)
  4824     232      have hodd : ¬ ((3 ^ a * 2 ^ (m'+1) - 1) % 2 = 0) := by rw [hM]; omega
  4825     233      rw [oddSteps, if_neg hodd, col_pred_pow, ih (a+1) m' (by omega)]
  4826     234      omega

  4828     236  /-- The all-odd class survives every depth up to `k`: no level of the split
  4829     237      reaches it. -/
  4830     238  theorem survives_pred_two_pow (k : ℕ) (hk : 1 ≤ k) : Survives k (2 ^ k - 1) := by

  4832     240    unfold Contracts
  4833     241    have h1 : oddSteps (2 ^ k - 1) i = i := by

  4849     257  /-! ## The survivor tree never dies
  4850     258
  4851     259  Every survivor class lifts to a survivor one level deeper.  The odd extension is
  4852     260  the one that always works: an odd step multiplies the multiplier by three while
  4853     261  the divisor only doubles, so a class that has not yet contracted cannot contract
  4854     262  by taking an odd step.  Since `3 ^ j` is odd, exactly one of the two lifts of a
  4855     263  class has an odd step at level `k`, and that one survives. -/
  4856     264
  4857     265  theorem oddSteps_succ : ∀ (k r : ℕ),
  4858     266      oddSteps r (k+1) = oddSteps r k + (if col^[k] r % 2 = 0 then 0 else 1) := by
  4859     267    intro k
  4860     268    induction k with
  4861     269    | zero => intro r; by_cases h : r % 2 = 0 <;> simp [oddSteps, h]
  4862     270    | succ k ih =>
  4863     271      intro r
  4864     272      have e1 : oddSteps r (k+1+1) = (if r % 2 = 0 then 0 else 1) + oddSteps (col r) (k+1) := by
  4865     273        rw [oddSteps]
  4866     274      have e2 : oddSteps r (k+1) = (if r % 2 = 0 then 0 else 1) + oddSteps (col r) k := by
  4867     275        rw [oddSteps]
  4868     276      rw [e1, ih (col r), e2, Function.iterate_succ_apply]
  4869     277      omega
  4870     278
  4871     279  theorem contracts_mod_le {i k : ℕ} (b r : ℕ) (h : i ≤ k) :
  4872     280      Contracts i (2 ^ k * b + r) ↔ Contracts i r := by
  4873     281    have hsplit : (2:ℕ) ^ k * b = 2 ^ i * (2 ^ (k - i) * b) := by
  4874     282      rw [← mul_assoc, ← pow_add]
  4875     283      congr 2
  4876     284      omega
  4877     285    rw [hsplit]
  4878     286    exact contracts_mod i _ r
  4879     287
  4880     288  /-- A survivor whose next step is odd survives one level deeper. -/
  4881     289  theorem survives_succ_of_odd {k r : ℕ} (h : Survives k r) (hodd : ¬ (col^[k] r % 2 = 0)) :
  4882     290      Survives (k+1) r := by
  4883     291    intro i hi
  4884     292    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge i (k+1) with hik | hik
  4885     293    · exact h i (by omega)
  4886     294    · have hik' : i = k + 1 := by omega
  4887     295      subst hik'
  4888     296      have hprev : ¬ Contracts k r := h k (le_refl k)
  4889     297      unfold Contracts at hprev ⊢
  4890     298      rw [oddSteps_succ, if_neg hodd]
  4891     299      have h3 : (2:ℕ) ^ k ≤ 3 ^ oddSteps r k := by omega
  4892     300      have hA : (2:ℕ) ^ (k+1) = 2 ^ k * 2 := pow_succ 2 k
  4893     301      have hB : (3:ℕ) ^ (oddSteps r k + 1) = 3 ^ oddSteps r k * 3 := pow_succ 3 _
  4894     302      have hC : (2:ℕ) ^ k * 2 ≤ 3 ^ oddSteps r k * 2 := Nat.mul_le_mul h3 (Nat.le_refl 2)
  4895     303      have hD : (3:ℕ) ^ oddSteps r k * 2 ≤ 3 ^ oddSteps r k * 3 :=
  4896     304        Nat.mul_le_mul (Nat.le_refl _) (by norm_num)
  4897     305      omega
  4898     306
  4899     307  /-- Every survivor class at depth `k` lifts to a survivor at depth `k+1`. -/
  4900     308  theorem exists_survivor_lift {k r : ℕ} (h : Survives k r) :
  4901     309      ∃ b, b < 2 ∧ Survives (k+1) (2 ^ k * b + r) := by
  4902     310    have hodd3 : (3:ℕ) ^ oddSteps r k % 2 = 1 := Nat.odd_iff.mp (Odd.pow (by decide))
  4903     311    by_cases hp : col^[k] r % 2 = 0
  4904     312    · refine ⟨1, by norm_num, ?_⟩
  4905     313      have hsurv : Survives k (2 ^ k * 1 + r) := by
  4906     314        intro i hi
  4907     315        rw [contracts_mod_le 1 r hi]
  4908     316        exact h i hi
  4909     317      refine survives_succ_of_odd hsurv ?_
  4910     318      rw [col_shift]
  4911     319      omega
  4912     320    · refine ⟨0, by norm_num, ?_⟩
  4913     321      have hsurv : Survives k (2 ^ k * 0 + r) := by
  4914     322        intro i hi
  4915     323        rw [contracts_mod_le 0 r hi]
  4916     324        exact h i hi
  4917     325      refine survives_succ_of_odd hsurv ?_
  4918     326      rw [col_shift]
  4919     327      omega
  4920     328
  4921     329  /-- No depth exhausts the split: a surviving class exists at every `k`. -/
  4922     330  theorem survivors_nonempty : ∀ k : ℕ, ∃ r, Survives k r := by
  4923     331    intro k
  4924     332    induction k with
  4925     333    | zero =>
  4926     334      refine ⟨1, ?_⟩
  4927     335      intro i hi
  4928     336      have hi0 : i = 0 := by omega
  4929     337      subst hi0
  4930     338      unfold Contracts
  4931     339      simp [oddSteps]
  4932     340    | succ k ih =>
  4933     341      obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := ih
  4934     342      obtain ⟨b, _, hb⟩ := exists_survivor_lift hr
  4935     343      exact ⟨2 ^ k * b + r, hb⟩
  4936     344
  4937     345  /-- A survivor with slack survives the next level whatever its next step is, so
  4938     346      both of its lifts survive.  The census reads this as a branch factor of
  4939     347      exactly two at every depth where the least admissible odd-count does not
  4940     348      advance. -/
  4941     349  theorem survives_succ_of_slack {k r : ℕ} (h : Survives k r)
  4942     350      (hslack : 2 ^ (k+1) ≤ 3 ^ oddSteps r k) : Survives (k+1) r := by
  4943     351    intro i hi
  4944     352    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge i (k+1) with hik | hik
  4945     353    · exact h i (by omega)
  4946     354    · have hik' : i = k + 1 := by omega
  4947     355      subst hik'
  4948     356      unfold Contracts
  4949     357      rw [oddSteps_succ]
  4950     358      by_cases hp : col^[k] r % 2 = 0
  4951     359      · rw [if_pos hp, Nat.add_zero]; omega
  4952     360      · rw [if_neg hp]
  4953     361        have hB : (3:ℕ) ^ (oddSteps r k + 1) = 3 ^ oddSteps r k * 3 := pow_succ 3 _
  4954     362        have hD : (3:ℕ) ^ oddSteps r k * 1 ≤ 3 ^ oddSteps r k * 3 :=
  4955     363          Nat.mul_le_mul (Nat.le_refl _) (by norm_num)
  4956     364        omega
  4957     365
  4958     366  theorem both_lifts_survive {k r : ℕ} (h : Survives k r)
  4959     367      (hslack : 2 ^ (k+1) ≤ 3 ^ oddSteps r k) (b : ℕ) :
  4960     368      Survives (k+1) (2 ^ k * b + r) := by
  4961     369    have hsurv : Survives k (2 ^ k * b + r) := by
  4962     370      intro i hi
  4963     371      rw [contracts_mod_le b r hi]
  4964     372      exact h i hi
  4965     373    refine survives_succ_of_slack hsurv ?_
  4966     374    rw [oddSteps_mod]
  4967     375    exact hslack
  4968     376
  4969     377  /-! ## The constant: the class criterion is exact
  4970     378
  4971     379  Every step is at least a multiplication by `3/2` on odd and exactly `1/2` on
  4972     380  even, so `2^k * col^[k] r ≥ 3^(oddSteps r k) * r` with no error term.  A class
  4973     381  that has not contracted has `3^j ≥ 2^k`, so its constant satisfies
  4974     382  `col^[k] r ≥ r`, and then no member of the class descends within `k` steps at
  4975     383  all.  The multiplier test is therefore not a sufficient condition with a finite
  4976     384  exceptional set: it is exact. -/
  4977     385
  4978     386  theorem pow_mul_le_iterate : ∀ (k r : ℕ), 3 ^ oddSteps r k * r ≤ 2 ^ k * col^[k] r := by
  4979     387    intro k
  4980     388    induction k with
  4981     389    | zero => intro r; simp [oddSteps]
  4982     390    | succ k ih =>
  4983     391      intro r
  4984     392      have hy := ih r
  4985     393      set y := col^[k] r with hydef
  4986     394      rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply', ← hydef, oddSteps_succ, ← hydef]
  4987     395      by_cases hp : y % 2 = 0
  4988     396      · have hcol : col y = y / 2 := by unfold col; rw [if_pos hp]
  4989     397        have hhalf : 2 * (y / 2) = y := by omega
  4990     398        rw [if_pos hp, Nat.add_zero, hcol, pow_succ]
  4991     399        calc 3 ^ oddSteps r k * r ≤ 2 ^ k * y := hy
  4992     400          _ = 2 ^ k * 2 * (y / 2) := by rw [mul_assoc, hhalf]

  5015     411  /-- A class that has not contracted carries a constant no smaller than its
  5016     412      residue. -/
  5017     413  theorem le_iterate_of_not_contracts {k r : ℕ} (h : ¬ Contracts k r) : r ≤ col^[k] r := by
  5018     414    unfold Contracts at h
  5019     415    have hge : (2:ℕ) ^ k ≤ 3 ^ oddSteps r k := by omega

  5022     418    have hpos : 0 < (2:ℕ) ^ k := pow_pos (by norm_num) k
  5023     419    have : (2:ℕ) ^ k * r ≤ 2 ^ k * col^[k] r := le_trans hstep hmain
  5024     420    exact Nat.le_of_mul_le_mul_left this hpos

  5026     422  /-- Nothing in a surviving class descends within `k` steps: the multiplier test

  5028     424  theorem no_member_descends {k r : ℕ} (h : ¬ Contracts k r) (t : ℕ) :

  5037     433  /-! ## The branch dichotomy
  5038     434
  5039     435  `survives_succ_of_slack` says a survivor with `2^(k+1) ≤ 3^j` keeps both lifts.
  5040     436  Its complement is exact: a survivor without that slack keeps only the lift whose
  5041     437  next step is odd, because the even lift leaves the multiplier where it was while
  5042     438  the divisor doubles past it.  So the census obeys `S_(k+1) = 2*A_k + B_k`, with
  5043     439  `A` the survivors carrying slack and `B` those without. -/
  5044     440
  5045     441  theorem contracts_succ_of_even_step {k r : ℕ} (heven : col^[k] r % 2 = 0)
  5046     442      (hslack : 3 ^ oddSteps r k < 2 ^ (k+1)) : Contracts (k+1) r := by
  5047     443    unfold Contracts
  5048     444    rw [oddSteps_succ, if_pos heven, Nat.add_zero]
  5049     445    exact hslack
  5050     446
  5051     447  /-- The even lift of a slackless survivor does not survive. -/
  5052     448  theorem not_survives_succ_of_even_step {k r : ℕ} (heven : col^[k] r % 2 = 0)
  5053     449      (hslack : 3 ^ oddSteps r k < 2 ^ (k+1)) : ¬ Survives (k+1) r := by
  5054     450    intro hs
  5055     451    exact hs (k+1) (le_refl _) (contracts_succ_of_even_step heven hslack)
  5056     452
  5057     453  /-! ## The holder-first reading: predecessors
  5058     454
  5059     455  Forward, the branch is free — both parities are available at every class, so the
  5060     456  tree is all `2^k` residues with a survival filter laid over it, and the filter is
  5061     457  all the content there is.  Read from the target instead and the branch becomes
  5062     458  arithmetic.  Every `m` has the predecessor `2*m`, and `m` has an odd predecessor
  5063     459  exactly when `m ≡ 2 (mod 3)`, in which case it is `2*t+1` for `m = 3*t+2` with no
  5064     460  division and no parity side condition.  Nothing needs filtering: every path in
  5065     461  this tree is a trajectory. -/
  5066     462
  5067     463  theorem col_two_mul (m : ℕ) : col (2 * m) = m := by
  5068     464    unfold col
  5069     465    rw [if_pos (by omega : (2 * m) % 2 = 0)]
  5070     466    omega
  5071     467
  5072     468  theorem col_odd_pred (t : ℕ) : col (2 * t + 1) = 3 * t + 2 := by
  5073     469    unfold col
  5074     470    rw [if_neg (by omega : ¬ (2 * t + 1) % 2 = 0)]
  5075     471    omega
  5076     472
  5077     473  /-- Every preimage is one of the two, and the odd one forces `m ≡ 2 (mod 3)`. -/
  5078     474  theorem preimage_cases {n m : ℕ} (h : col n = m) :
  5079     475      n = 2 * m ∨ ∃ t, n = 2 * t + 1 ∧ m = 3 * t + 2 := by

  5081     477    · obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := he
  5082     478      left
  5083     479      have hn : n % 2 = 0 := by omega
  5084     480      unfold col at h
  5085     481      rw [if_pos hn] at h

  5089     485      refine ⟨t, ht, ?_⟩
  5090     486      subst ht
  5091     487      rw [col_odd_pred] at h

  5217     601  /-- The chain runs on `1 (mod 3)` and stops on `0 (mod 3)`: the lift `(4n-1)/3`
  5218     602      is an integer exactly there. -/
  5219     603  theorem chain_defined_iff (n : ℕ) : (∃ t, n = 3 * t + 1) ↔ n % 3 = 1 := by
  5220     604    constructor
  5221     605    · rintro ⟨t, rfl⟩; omega
  5222     606    · intro h; exact ⟨n / 3, by omega⟩

  5224     608  /-! ## The junctions
  5225     609
  5226     610  A value takes two predecessors exactly when it is `2 (mod 3)` (`odd_pred_iff`),
  5227     611  so every merge of two trajectories happens there and nowhere else.  Which arm the
  5228     612  traffic arrives on is then forced, and the forcing is already in `col_odd_pred`:
  5229     613  an odd step sends `2t+1` to `3t+2`, which is `2 (mod 3)` for every `t`.  So EVERY
  5230     614  odd step lands on a junction, while an even step lands on one only when its own
  5231     615  half does — a third of the time.  With the two step kinds equinumerous that puts
  5232     616  the odd arm's share of all junction arrivals at `(1/2) / (1/2 + (1/3)(1/2)) = 3/4`,
  5233     617  and the census reads `0.7513` over seeds to 20000 and `0.7501` to 60000. -/
  5234     618
  5235     619  /-- Every odd step lands on a junction. -/
  5236     620  theorem odd_step_lands_on_junction (t : ℕ) : col (2 * t + 1) % 3 = 2 := by
  5237     621    rw [col_odd_pred]; omega
  5238     622
  5239     623  /-- An even step lands on a junction exactly when its half is one, which is a
  5240     624      third of the residues. -/
  5241     625  theorem even_step_junction_iff (m : ℕ) : col (2 * m) % 3 = 2 ↔ m % 3 = 2 := by
  5242     626    rw [col_two_mul]
  5243     627
  5244     628  /-- The two arms into a junction, named: the even arm is always there, the odd
  5245     629      arm is the one the residue buys. -/
  5246     630  theorem junction_arms {v : ℕ} (h : v % 3 = 2) :
  5247     631      col (2 * v) = v ∧ col (2 * (v / 3) + 1) = v :=
  5248     632    ⟨col_two_mul v, by rw [col_odd_pred]; omega⟩
  5249     633
  5250     634  /-! ## What fixes the share: the starved arm
  5251     635
  5252     636  Grouping junctions by residue, the odd share is fixed one 3-adic digit at a time.
  5253     637  At modulus 9 the class `5` is pinned at `0.004` with a spread of `0.0014` while
  5254     638  the other two classes spread over a quarter; at modulus 27 the pinned classes are
  5255     639  `5, 14, 23` and at 81 the spreads elsewhere fall to a few hundredths.
  5256     640
  5257     641  The pinned class has an exact cause. A junction `v = 9k+5` has odd arm `6k+3`,
  5258     642  which is `0 (mod 3)`, and a value `0 (mod 3)` takes only its doubling
  5259     643  predecessor — `preds_of_barren` — while every value in its doubling chain stays
  5260     644  `0 (mod 3)` — `barren_doubling`. So that arm is a bare chain of `d+1` nodes at
  5261     645  depth `d` against the even arm's exponential subtree, and the share it can hold
  5262     646  falls like `d / (4/3)^d`. At depth 24 that is `0.004`, which is what the census
  5263     647  reads. -/
  5264     648
  5265     649  theorem col_starved_arm (k : ℕ) : col (6 * k + 3) = 9 * k + 5 := by
  5266     650    unfold col
  5267     651    rw [if_neg (by omega : ¬ (6 * k + 3) % 2 = 0)]
  5268     652    omega
  5269     653
  5270     654  /-- A value divisible by three has one predecessor, not two. -/
  5271     655  theorem preds_of_barren {u n : ℕ} (hu : u % 3 = 0) (h : col n = u) : n = 2 * u := by
  5272     656    rcases preimage_cases h with h1 | ⟨t, _, h2⟩
  5273     657    · exact h1
  5274     658    · omega
  5275     659
  5276     660  /-- And its whole doubling chain stays barren, so the arm never branches. -/
  5277     661  theorem barren_doubling (u i : ℕ) (hu : u % 3 = 0) : (2 ^ i * u) % 3 = 0 := by
  5278     662    obtain ⟨m, rfl⟩ : ∃ m, u = 3 * m := ⟨u / 3, by omega⟩
  5279     663    have : 2 ^ i * (3 * m) = 3 * (2 ^ i * m) := by ring
  5280     664    omega
  5281     665
  5282     666  /-- The junction whose odd arm is barren, named by its residue. -/
  5283     667  theorem starved_junction (k : ℕ) :
  5284     668      (9 * k + 5) % 3 = 2 ∧ col (6 * k + 3) = 9 * k + 5 ∧ (6 * k + 3) % 3 = 0 :=
  5285     669    ⟨by omega, col_starved_arm k, by omega⟩

  5288     672

  5290     674  `(4/3)^d`, so what separates one arm from another is the constant in front of it.
  5291     675  Measure

  5401     785  It is also NOT `(4/3)^d`.  The gap between the Fibonacci ceiling and the measured
  5402     786  `4/3` is exactly the assumption that the odd child's class is equidistributed
  5403     787  rather than adversarial: taking the odd child to land on `2 (mod 3)` every time
  5404     788  gives `φ`, and taking it uniformly over the three classes gives `4/3`.  So the
  5405     789  amplitude's boundedness is not a consequence of the residue structure, and the
  5406     790  measurement that stands behind it — `gap × length` flat at `−1.3` across two
  5407     791  orders of magnitude — is evidence for the equidistribution rather than a
  5408     792  substitute for it. -/

  5461     835  /-- The pairing IS the Fibonacci recursion, shifted by two. -/
  5462     836  theorem classBound_fib (d : ℕ) :
  5463     837      (classBound (d + 2)).2 = (classBound (d + 1)).2 + (classBound d).2 + 2 := by

  5466     840
  5467     841  /-! ## Toward the equidistribution
  5468     842
  5469     843  The odd lift is a bijection from the junction classes one digit finer onto
  5470     844  everything.  Concretely a junction is `3t+2` and its odd arm is `2t+1`, so on
  5471     845  residues the arm map is `t ↦ 2t+1`, which is injective and onto `Z/3^r` because
  5472     846  `2` is a unit there.  A junction class mod `3^(r+1)` is a `t` class mod `3^r`, so
  5473     847  the arm carries the finer classes onto the coarser ones one-to-one: uniform in
  5474     848  gives uniform out, and the digit is what it costs.
  5475     849
  5476     850  That is the whole of what the structure gives for free.  It says the uniform
  5477     851  measure is FIXED by the level map; it does not say the tree converges to it,
  5478     852  because the tree starts at a point and every level consumes a digit rather than
  5479     853  producing one.  The convergence is the open piece, and the shape it has is a
  5480     854  flow between conductors: doubling permutes the characters of a fixed conductor,
  5481     855  while the odd arm sends a conductor `3^r` character to one of conductor `3^(r+1)`,
  5482     856  so the coefficient at each level is fed from the level above it and never from
  5483     857  below. -/
  5484     858
  5485     859  /-- The arm map on residues is injective. -/
  5486     860  theorem arm_inj_mod {r a b : ℕ} (h : (2 * a + 1) % 3 ^ r = (2 * b + 1) % 3 ^ r) :
  5487     861      a % 3 ^ r = b % 3 ^ r :=
  5488     862    odd_map_inj_mod h
  5489     863
  5490     864  /-- And onto: every residue is `2t+1` for some `t`, by the explicit inverse of 2. -/
  5491     865  theorem arm_surj_mod (r y : ℕ) :
  5492     866      ∃ t, (2 * t + 1) % 3 ^ r = y % 3 ^ r := by
  5493     867    have hpos : 1 ≤ 3 ^ r := Nat.one_le_pow r 3 (by norm_num)
  5494     868    have hodd : Odd (3 ^ r) := Odd

  5605     979  theorem arm_collide_iff {r s t : ℕ} :

  5612     986  /-! ## Why the cross correlation carries a sign
  5613     987
  5614     988  Doubling is an involution on the live classes mod 3: `2·1 ≡ 2` and `2·2 ≡ 1`, so
  5615     989  the two classes that branch are swapped every level and returned every second
  5616     990  level.  An imbalance between them therefore tends to alternate in sign, and the
  5617     991  correlation between a level and its own image under the arms is negative for that
  5618     992  reason rather than by accident.
  5619     993
  5620     994  Measured, the bias is real and strongest where the swap is purest.  The cross
  5621     995  deviation is negative in 20 of 24 levels at conductor 3 with mean `−0.140`, 15 of
  5622     996  22 at conductor 9 with mean `−0.063`, and 12 of 20 at conductor 27 with mean
  5623     997  `−0.037` — weakening as the conductor rises and the swap dilutes.  The signed
  5624     998  imbalance at conductor 3 changes sign every two to three levels rather than every
  5625     999  level, which is the involution perturbed by the odd arm's feed. -/
  5626    1000

  5639    1001  /-- Doubling swaps the two live classes and fixes the dead one. -/
  5640    1002  theorem double_swaps_classes (m : ℕ) :
  5641    1003      (m % 3 = 1 → (2 * m) % 3 = 2) ∧ (m % 3 = 2 → (2 * m) % 3 = 1)
  5642    1004        ∧ (m % 3 = 0 → (2 * m) % 3 = 0) := by
  5643    1005    refine ⟨fun h => by omega, fun h => by omega, fun h => by omega⟩
  5644    1006
  5645    1007  /-- And returns every value to its own class after two steps, so doubling is an
  5646    1008      involution on residues mod 3. -/
  5647    1009  theorem double_involution (m : ℕ) : (4 * m) % 3 = m % 3 := by omega
  5648    1010
  5649    1011  /-! ## The perturbation, exactly
  5650    1012
  5651    1013  At conductor three the even children swap the two live classes, so their whole
  5652    1014  contribution to the imbalance `n₁ − n₂` is its negation.  The odd children come
  5653    1015  only from the junctions, and which class they land in is fixed by the parent's
  5654    1016  residue mod 9: `2 ↦ 1`, `5 ↦ 0`, `8 ↦ 2`.  Only two of those three touch the
  5655    1017  imbalance, so
  5656    1018
  5657    1019      I_{d+1} = −I_d + (m₂ − m₈)
  5658    1020
  5659    1021  with `m_c` the level's counts mod 9.  The involution is the minus sign and the
  5660    1022  perturbation is one difference of two mod-9 classes, nothing else.
  5661    1023
  5662    1024  Checked in exact integers, the identity holds at every level to depth 30 with a
  5663    1025  single exception at level 2, where the tree cuts its own `1 → 2 → 1` edge and the
  5664    1026  prediction of 2 meets an actual 1 — off by exactly the omitted node.  The
  5665    1027  perturbation is not small: its mean size is `1.79` times the imbalance it
  5666    1028  perturbs, so the odd arm carries the level rather than nudging it, while the
  5667    1029  imbalance itself stays at a fraction of `√N`. -/
  5668    1030
  5669    1031  theorem odd_child_class_two (v : ℕ) (h : v % 9 = 2) : (2 * (v / 3) + 1) % 3 = 1 := by
  5670    1032    omega

=== checking hosted runner ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# run_hosted_cmds.sh — feed REPL commands to the HOSTED binary, no QEMU.
#
# The non-interactive sibling of run_hosted.sh, and the counterpart of
# run_serial_cmds.sh: same arguments, same transcript, without the boot. A QEMU
# boot dominates the cost of any short command, which is why run_serial_cmds.sh
# tells callers to batch. Here there is nothing to amortise.
#
# Usage: ./run_hosted_cmds.sh "sic d16" "weight ⊢∈⊤◻⊣" ...
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")"

PROFILE="${PROFILE:-release}"
BIN="target/x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu/${PROFILE}/momonados"

if [ ! -x "$BIN" ]; then
  PROFILE_FLAG=()
  [ "$PROFILE" = "release" ] && PROFILE_FLAG=(--release)
  cargo build "${PROFILE_FLAG[@]}" --target x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu \
    --features hosted >&2
fi

{ for c in "$@"; do printf '%s\n' "$c"; done; printf 'quit\n'; } | "$BIN"

[INFO]   ACT:   run_command({"command": "cd /home/mrnob0dy666/imsgct && cat MoDoT/ask 2>/dev/null | head -60 && echo \"=== Checking for imasm compose/path/chaos tools ===\" && ls -la MoDoT/imasm* 2>/dev/null && ls -la imasmic_core/ 2>/dev/null | head -20", "timeout": 15})
[INFO]   OBS:   #!/usr/bin/env bash
# ask — colour on the screen, clean text in the file, without anyone typing a pipe.
#
# The style layer turns colour off when stdout is not a terminal, which is right:
# escapes have no business in a log or in what the agent reads back. But it meant
# every `| tee` run lost colour, and the fix for that kept landing on the person
# running it. So it lands here instead.
#
#   ask …                 → colour, as any terminal program
#   ask … --log FILE      → colour on screen AND a clean copy in FILE
#   ask … | anything      → bare, unchanged
BIN="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)/ask.bin"

log=""
args=()
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --log) log="$2"; shift 2 ;;
    --log=*) log="${1#--log=}"; shift ;;
    *) args+=("$1"); shift ;;
  esac
done

if [ -z "$log" ]; then
  exec "$BIN" "${args[@]}"
fi

# `script` hands the program a pty, so it still sees a terminal and still colours;
# the sed strips the escapes on the way to the file only.
cmd=$(printf '%q ' "$BIN" "${args[@]}")
script -qec "$cmd" /dev/null | tee >(sed -u -e 's/\x1b\[[0-9;]*m//g' -e 's/\r$//' > "$log")

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# run_hosted_cmds.sh — feed REPL commands to the HOSTED binary, no QEMU.
#
# The non-interactive sibling of run_hosted.sh, and the counterpart of
# run_serial_cmds.sh: same arguments, same transcript, without the boot. A QEMU
# boot dominates the cost of any short command, which is why run_serial_cmds.sh
# tells callers to batch. Here there is nothing to amortise.
#
# Usage: ./run_hosted_cmds.sh "sic d16" "weight ⊢∈⊤◻⊣" ...
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")"

PROFILE="${PROFILE:-release}"
BIN="target/x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu/${PROFILE}/momonados"

if [ ! -x "$BIN" ]; then
  PROFILE_FLAG=()
  [ "$PROFILE" = "release" ] && PROFILE_FLAG=(--release)
  cargo build "${PROFILE_FLAG[@]}" --target x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu \
    --features hosted >&2
fi

{ for c in "$@"; do printf '%s\n' "$c"; done; printf 'quit\n'; } | "$BIN"
